# List of flags

Please help with verifying or updating older sections of this article.
At least some were last verified for [version](/Europa_Universalis_5_Wiki%3AVersioning "Europa Universalis 5 Wiki:Versioning") 1.0.

*See also: [Category:Flags](/Category%3AFlags "Category:Flags")*

Many countries in the game can have different flags depending on the circumstances. If the conditions for multiple flags are met, the flag with the highest priority is used. This list shows the flags without the flag of the overlord which is often included in a corner if the [game rule](/Game_rule "Game rule") *“Flags for subjects”* is active. Flags which are listed multiple time have multiple entries in /Europa Universalis V/game/in\_game/common/flag\_definitions/ (usually with different conditions or different rules about where to show the flag of the overlord). Countries which have only one flag are not included in this list, because they can be found in the [list of countries](/Countries "Countries"). Generated flags are similarly not included.

The overlord canton`subject_canton` in the game script is the flag applied to this country's subjects' flags, when allowed by the subject's current flag. The subject flag`coa_with_overlord_canton` in the game script is the flag used when this country is a subject. Most often, one or both of these are identical to the country's main flag. Some countries have special subject flags for use when the subject of certain countries such as Great Britain or France. The subject of a subject uses its top overlord's canton when allowed.

The [![Flag of America](/thumb.php?f=Flag_USA.png&width=24)](/America "America") [United States of America](/America "America") has a large number of flags that represent the number of areas with cores. These flags all have priority equal to their number of stars and starting with 100, from flag 14 at priority 10014 to flag 100 at priority 10100. They all allow for an overlord canton to be applied.
